# E1-T04 · Impersonation grant and handoff between apps

## Objective
Work package WP-01-C: «Entra com l'abonat» tokens for admins, and the one-time code that moves a session from `clubs` to `clubs-admin` (and back).

## Spec references
S01 R-01-09 (impersonation), R-01-13 (handoff), R-01-17 · tests **T-01-11, T-01-12** · `docs/specs/S14-*.md` (audit of impersonated actions) · `docs/specs/00-transversal/MATRIU_PERMISOS.md`.

## Context to load
S01 R-01-09, R-01-13, §6 rows `/members/{id}/impersonation-token`, `/auth/handoff`, `/oauth2/token` handoff grant · S14 §5 (`impersonatedMemberId` in AuditEntry, E0-T07) · this file.

## Steps
1. `POST /members/{id}/impersonation-token` (ADMIN of the same club; a token that is itself impersonated → `403 IMPERSONATION_DENIED`): checks `Member ACTIVE/INACTIVE` + its `Membership` (R-01-09), creates `ImpersonationGrant` (collection, `auth.impersonationMinutes` = 60, `reason`), issues an access JWT **without refresh token** with `sub` = the member's account, `roles [MEMBER]`, claims `imp = true`, `actorAccountId`, `impersonatedMemberId`; event `ImpersonationStarted{actorAccountId, memberId}` (catalog) + audit `IMPERSONATION_STARTED{reason}` in the same transaction; `POST /oauth2/revoke` of that token → `ImpersonationEnded`. Response per S01/S03 §6 (aligned 06-09): `201 {token, expiresAt, launchUrl}` where `launchUrl = https://{clubs-app host of the club}/entrar?handoff=<code>` and the code is a single-use 60 s handoff code (step 3 mechanism) bound to the impersonated session and the `clubs-app` client — the JWT itself never travels in a URL; the web opens `launchUrl` in a new tab (E2-W02 already consumes it). `ADMIN`/`INSTRUCTOR` endpoints reject `imp = true` with `403 FORBIDDEN` (a `@RejectsImpersonation` guard or the existing role resolver).
2. Request filter: when `imp` is present, `CurrentUser.impersonation()` is set so every audited mutation records `actorAccountId` and `impersonatedMemberId` (E0-T07 `AuditWriter` reads it); expose `CurrentUser.origin()` = `DomainEvent.Origin.BACKOFFICE` for S08 (the model's `origin` enum is `APP · BACKOFFICE · INSTRUCTOR · SYSTEM`; an impersonated write is `BACKOFFICE` with both ids in the audit entry).
3. Handoff R-01-13: `POST /auth/handoff {targetClientId}` (authenticated) → single-use code (60 s, hashed) bound to the account, the club context and the target client; grant `urn:agilityhub:grant:handoff` exchanges it for tokens of the target client; reuse → `400 HANDOFF_INVALID` + `SecurityEvent HANDOFF_INVALID`; `403 NO_MEMBERSHIP` when the account lacks the target role. The web lands on `https://{target host}/entrar?handoff=<code>` (R-01-13) — the code is only ever in that query parameter, never logged.
4. `POST /oauth2/revoke` accepts impersonation tokens.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` → green with T-01-11 (impersonation claims + an audited mutation carrying `actorAccountId`) and T-01-12 (handoff single use, 60 s, wrong client).

## Definition of Done
Both grants implemented and audited · security events written.

## Evidence to return
`verify` output; decoded impersonation JWT (fictional data).

---

Completed E1-T04. The working tree contains both grants, tenant/role guards, request identity propagation, transactional impersonation audit/outbox, and the updated OpenAPI contract.

No git write commands were run. The session publisher owns commits and pushes on `main`.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- R-01-09 / T-01-11: ADMIN of the same club; ACTIVE/INACTIVE target with matching active MEMBER membership; member-only claims, no platform privileges, no refresh token, configured expiry; nested impersonation denied; persisted grant checked on every impersonated request.
- R-01-09, S14 R-14-09 / T-01-11: `@Audited(IMPERSONATION_STARTED)` and `ImpersonationStarted` are committed with the grant. The test-only audited mutation records the administrator as `actorAccountId` plus `impersonatedMemberId`, and obtains `BACKOFFICE` through `CurrentUser.origin()`. A forced rollback leaves no grant, audit or event. `@AuditCovers` registers the new action.
- R-01-09 / T-01-11: ADMIN/INSTRUCTOR routes reject genuine impersonation JWTs; `/me` exposes MEMBER-only profiles plus actor name. Revocation is ownership-scoped, idempotent and emits `ImpersonationEnded` once. Tests cover altered signed claims, missing/cross-tenant members, invalid linkage/status/roles, loss of actor role and expiration.
- R-01-13 / T-01-12: 256-bit single-use codes stored as SHA-256 digests; 60-second expiry; target client, account, club and source session binding; registered verified HTTPS destination `/entrar?handoff=`. Wrong client/tenant, reuse, expiry, lost role/membership, source revocation and account family-version changes are denied. Concurrent redemption issues exactly one destination session; rollback preserves the code.
- R-01-17 / T-01-11, T-01-12: anonymous requests and cross-tenant bearer use are rejected; refresh tokens remain client-bound; no request accepts a `clubId` body parameter.
- Extended event/audit catalog contracts and existing identity/E2/security MVC contracts for the now-implemented grants. Added `HANDOFF_INVALID` telemetry as explicitly required by task step 3. Full `verify` checks architecture, unit/integration tests, OpenAPI and coverage thresholds.

### Assumptions
- Dependency E1-T02 is verified. Exactly one roadmap task was implemented; no other task file or Organizer verification section was edited.
- Full S03 persistence and S08 bookings are future work. A narrow census read projection behind `MemberIdentityAccess` supplies the target check without coupling identity to census internals or adding a census write API. The audited mutation test is an explicit stand-in for S08's future use of `CurrentUser`.
- S01's ACTIVE/INACTIVE wording is applied literally; the current census contract also names LEFT, which is denied. The projection does not introduce or alter a census status enum.
- Existing public clients `clubs-app` and `clubs-admin` map to verified `Club.domains.app` values `clubs` and `clubs-admin`; primary domain wins, with deterministic host order otherwise. Missing verified destinations use existing `UNKNOWN_HOST`. The global OAuth endpoint resolves tenant solely from possession of the hashed code; an explicit conflicting club host is rejected.
- Handoff requires a live ordinary source session and checks the destination role both at creation and exchange. `clubs-app` accepts any active club membership because ADMIN and INSTRUCTOR also use its profile-selection flow. Impersonation cannot create ordinary sessions via handoff/profile or manage a member's real sessions.
- A revoked impersonation bearer is accepted only for idempotently revoking that exact same token. All ordinary requests reject it; the actor can also revoke the grant with a normal session.
- `DomainEvent.Origin.INSTRUCTOR` is added from the task/model enum; existing `WEBHOOK` remains for compatibility. Impersonated writes use BACKOFFICE.

### Questions / catalog proposals
- See the two E1-T04 entries in `roadmap/MESSAGES.md`: align S03's future inactive status representation and add the task-authorized `HANDOFF_INVALID` security telemetry type to S14's list.
- No new parameter, notification, error code or domain-event name was proposed or silently invented. Successful handoff has no catalog audit action, so it creates no invented audit entry; invalid code use records the required SecurityEvent.
